<narrative>Prayers&#x2014;<recordedTime time="1988-02-09T14:15:00">14h15.</recordedTime></narrative>

</prayers>

<debateSection name="#referral_of_bill_and_memorandum_to_standing_committee">

<heading>REFERRAL OF BILL AND MEMORANDUM TO STANDING COMMITTEE</heading>

<summary>(Announcement)</summary>

<p>The CHAIRMAN announced that in terms of Rule 23 (4) the Acting Speaker had referred the following draft Bill which had been submitted to him, together with the memorandum thereon, to the Standing Committee on Private Members&#x2019; Draft Bills:</p>

<block name="quote"><i>Freedom of Farming Bill,</i> submitted by Mr J V Iyman.</block>

</debateSection>

<debateSection name="#motion_of_no_confidence_in_ministers_council">

<heading>MOTION OF NO CONFIDENCE IN MINISTERS&#x2019; COUNCIL</heading>

<summary>(Resumed)</summary>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, actually the motion should have read that the House has no confidence in the Cabinet and the junior partners of the Cabinet, viz the Ministers&#x2019; Council. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Did the hon member for Fish River mention a jackal?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#koeberg">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">C KOEBERG</person>:</from>

<p>No, Sir. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I appeal to hon members to conduct the debate in the right spirit, otherwise I shall have no choice but to request hon members who do not adhere to my request to leave the Chamber.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>As far as I am concerned today is an epoch-making day. For the first time a fully registered party is introducing a motion of no confidence in the Ministers&#x2019; Council in this House. In addition this is the first fully non-racial party to do so in the new dispensation. [Interjections.] If hon members regard that as a joke, they must come and prove to me that they are non-racial. A big fuss is sometimes made of the concept of non-racialism, but in the present rickety dispensation the United Democratic Party is the first non-racial party in the tricameral system. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>You are an &#x201C;Uncle Tom&#x201D;.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>It is the &#x201C;Uncle Toms&#x201D; that I am going to talk to today. I do not know why that hon member wants to anticipate my speech.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>I suppose you are the expert.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Yes, I am a great expert even if I did not study in California to get my degree.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Hon members must please not make personal remarks. Once hon members get a turn to speak, they can refute everything the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition has said. That is the correct way in which to debate. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>One can understand that hon members who are the &#x201C;Uncle Toms&#x201D; of this system will become very sensitive about these matters. [Interjections.] Sir, I am not going to take any further notice of hon members, because I want to continue my speech.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>Federal &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Yes, federal, but most of the Federal Party members are in the Labour Party today. We all know that, but I want to emphasise once again that until now Government policy has been based on discrimination.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#andrews">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">B J ANDREWS</person>:</from>

<p>You are part of that.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>We know that is a fact, even if they say it is differentiation. No matter on what basis they want to call it differentiation, it is a misplaced concept in the South African system. The so-called differentiation among people cries to high heaven. Hon members on the opposite side do not address this problem. The Government, particularly through the hon the State President, must say whether or not the unmentioned aspects of its policy are true. The Government must not <span class="col_325-326" refersTo="page_0185"/>say this to us in vague ambiguous terms, but must be very specific. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>It is an irrefutable fact that apartheid is heresy. I do not want to act like Paul Kruger&#x2019;s little monkey in the House this afternoon. Where, according to Government policy, of which the Ministers&#x2019; Council is a part &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#andrews">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">B J ANDREWS</person>:</from>

<p>You are also part of it.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I am not part of the executive authority. [Interjections.] The Ministers&#x2019; Council is the executive authority by order of the hon the State President. That is what it is all about.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>But you are PW&#x2019;s &#x201C;blue-eyed boy&#x201D;.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>If that hon member wants to make me the hon the State President&#x2019;s &#x201C;blue-eyed boy&#x201D;, he will get full marks for doing so. He will not manage it, however. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Addo can request a turn to speak.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>In the first place full citizenship within the state is being refused on the basis of race or nationality. [Interjections.] I shall re-emphasize that, because the hon member has so little between his ears that I do not know whether he always realises it.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#williams">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">A WILLIAMS</person>:</from>

<p>One cannot miss your ears.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Secondly, even membership of the church is subject to this concept. I do not even want to mention Holy Communion.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>Then you are one too.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I do not go, because I do not believe in heresy. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Bonteheuwel must please keep quiet.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Thirdly, free and unimpeded participation in a free economic system is prohibited if one is not of the right colour and there is no free participation in a single educational system because above all, race and nationality reign supreme.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>We say that, but PW does not.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I do not mind who says that, the hon the State President or anyone else. The fact is that the Ministers&#x2019; Council is part of the hon the State President&#x2019;s Government. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Addo must come to order. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>In the fifth place social matters such as marriages, involvement in political parties, cultural organisations, trade and trade unions, other social activities, as well as admission to public facilities, are still subject to race and nationality. What is more, Sir, they are subject to blatant domination.</p>

<p>In the sixth place one is not granted status with full dignity, rights and privileges, because&#x2014;once again!&#x2014;one&#x2019;s racial and national identity is not in line with that of the Whites, who ostensibly wield the sceptre. [Interjections.] I just want to tell the hon member of the ostrich region that I have always written my own speeches; no one else writes my speeches for me.</p>

<p>&#x2020;Mr Chairman, I stand before you&#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>As someone who went to a tea party. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>&#x2026; at the climax of intense skirmishing between the hon the State President and the hon Chairman of the Minister&#x2019;s Council, a junior partner of the Government. [Interjections.] He and the Labour Party can in no way be regarded as the largest opposition party within the tricameral system. They are a part of Government. [Interjections.] In fact, they are the Government, albeit for own affairs.</p>

<p>We of the UDP as well as the whole future of parliamentary activity&#x2014;in fact, democracy itself&#x2014;are being placed in jeopardy by a combination of posturing, paranoia, brinkmanship and subterfuge. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#jenneke">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">L J JENNEKE</person>:</from>

<p>That is ancient history!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from><span class="col_327-328" refersTo="page_0186"/>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Northern Cape must come to order. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>The only conclusion I can draw, Mr Chairman, is that the UDP and likeminded people&#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#koeberg">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">C KOEBERG</person>:</from>

<p>The drunken party.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Fish River must please withdraw the words &#x201C;drunken party&#x201D;.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#koeberg">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">C KOEBERG</person>:</from>

<p>I withdraw them unconditionally, Sir.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>The hon member must chase his wife around the house with a knife in the presence of the police again, then he will be able to display more sense. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Will the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition resume his seat for a moment. [Interjections.] Order! For the second time I should like to appeal to hon members on both sides not to make personal remarks. That is why I asked the hon member for Fish River to withdraw what he had said, and for the same reason I am asking the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition to withdraw what he said. The hon the Leader may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I shall withdraw what I said, Sir, but I have to answer the hon gentleman on the other side who asked where my wife was. My wife is at her home.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I appeal to hon members for the third time. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I knew they would carry on in that way, Sir. [Interjections.] They cannot tackle politics; they have to tackle the man. [Interjections.] But I am ready for them!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I</p>

<p>shall not permit personal remarks, and I am referring to hon members on both sides of the House!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I honour your ruling, Sir.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Thank you. The hon the Leader may proceed. [Interjections.] Order! The hon member for Addo must come to order!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I want to repeat what I said previously, Sir.</p>

<p>&#x2020;The only conclusion I can draw is that the UDP and likeminded people not only have the opportunity but also have the duty, the obligation, to become a major force in South African politics. [Interjections.] I thank God that the UDP is able to act and is seen to act in accordance with its principles, and to act only in the interests of all people of South Africa. The UDP&#x2019;s non-racial executive in Natal is proof of this and we are prepared to face reality in this regard. In South African terms, Blacks, Whites, Coloureds and Indians are welcome to join us&#x2014;as some have already done!</p>

<p>The UDP is not fettered by irrelevant and outmoded dogma or the need to appease anybody, be it the Government, or opposing forces for that matter. [Interjections.] We are free to pursue the rational dictates of our conscience without fear. We have already proved our good faith by rejecting the safe and the comfortable. We cannot be influenced by the need to hang on desperately in order to secure pensions and entitlements. [Interjections.] We are not the &#x201C;Non-National&#x201D; the &#x201C;Non-Labour Party&#x201D; or any &#x201C;non-party&#x201D; for that matter. This party has it its own existence and functions which are far too important to be described as &#x201C;non&#x201D; anything.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>You were born of the Labour Party.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I was born of the Federal Party. I made the Labour Party what it is. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! If the hon member for Wuppertal wants a turn to speak, he must request one. I refer to the hon member for Northern Cape for the second time as well. I request hon members to assist me and to <span class="col_329-330" refersTo="page_0187"/>maintain order. The hon the Leader may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, we are the UDP and we are dedicated to the same ideals on which the prosperous, liberal democracies are built. [Interjections.] I have been asked the question so let me now postulate the principles on which the UDP was founded. [Interjections.] These are: Firstly, the recognition of the inherent dignity of man and the equal and viable rights of all persons to justice and peace; secondly, one nation, one South Africa with universal suffrage in a multi-party dispensation; thirdly, the institutionalisation of basic human rights and the Rule of Law; fourthly, the achievement of a peaceful transition to a free and open society through democratic processes; and fifthly equal opportunity in the work place and the provision of housing.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#jenneke">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">L J JENNEKE</person>:</from>

<p>Yes, master.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Speaking of &#x201C;yes master&#x201D;, Sir, there has been so much talk this year of &#x201C;yes, master&#x201D;, &#x201C;no, master&#x201D;, &#x201C;excuse me, master&#x201D;, that I do not have to reply to that statement.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Did the hon the Minister of Health Services and Welfare use the word &#x201C;sies&#x201D;?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#minister_of_health_services_and_welfare">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">MINISTER OF HEALTH SERVICES AND WELFARE</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I said &#x201C;sies tog&#x201D;.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I do not reply to questions put by irrelevant people.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition need only refuse and say he is not replying to any questions. He need not qualify his refusal.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#jenneke">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">L J JENNEKE</person>:</from>

<p>He has no mandate.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I want to continue. In the sixth place we propagate an economic order which will satisfy the just aspirations of all South Africans. In the seventh place, we are committed to the repeal of all discriminatory legislation. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>That is Labour Party policy.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Hon members wanted me to expound our constitution, but now they do not want to accept the principles I have enunciated. If that hon member can quote what I said from the Labour Party&#x2019;s constitution then I shall agree with him. Sir, I continue. We also propagate the right to freedom of association, speech and religion, the upliftment of the general state of welfare and living, and the acceptance of the Freedom Charter. [Interjections.] We are the first party in the tricameral system to accept the Freedom Charter, because it is the people&#x2019;s charter.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#mckenzie">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P C McKENZIE</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, may I put a question to the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I am not replying to any questions, and that applies to everyone, Mr Chairman. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>&#x2020;I want to reiterate that we believe in the government of the people &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>You stand for violence!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>&#x2026; by the people, for the people and in terms of a fair and just constitution, with a bill of rights drawn up by the representatives of all South Africans, irrespective of their race, colour, creed or sex. So much, then, for the UDP.</p>

<p>*I now want to come back to the reactionary Ministers&#x2019; Council. It is our experience that no one wants to be seen with them in public. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>Sir, the fact that there is police protection at funerals, public meetings and elections confirms this. Casspirs were called in in Pietersburg so that they could enjoy the fresh air with the electorate. [Interjections.] That hon member was part of that. He will remember how they plagued the first executive of the old Coloured Representative Council about this police protection. They said they could go anywhere without police protection. [Interjections.] And now, Sir?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#mckenzie">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P C McKENZIE</person>:</from>

<p>Do you have police protection?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Now there are police in front, the Minister in the middle, and police at the rear&#x2014;to go and meet the electorate. [Interjections.] I have never had a single policeman to protect me <span class="col_331-332" refersTo="page_0188"/>because I was participating in this system. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I want to ask the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition whether he accepts that police protection was implemented in terms of the Act, and that therefore it is normal practice for police protection to be given to Ministers&#x2014;whether they are White, Coloured or Indian&#x2014;in their official capacity.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I have no problem with that, Mr Chairman. I do want to point out, however, how these hon members carried on when other people received police protection. To my mind that is what is at issue. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>What I find interesting, however, is that the members of the Coloured Representative Council&#x2019;s executive merely advised the Government, whereas these hon Ministers are part of the Government; they are not simply junior partners. Perhaps they are ineffectual partners. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>The hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council came to ask me whether I was interested in a promotional position, and I refused it. [Interjections.] I do not want to be an own affairs Minister. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Far too much reference is made these days to the Labour Party as the majority opposition party in Parliament.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#minister_of_health_services_and_welfare">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">MINISTER OF HEALTH SERVICES AND WELFARE</person>:</from>

<p>But that is the case!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>On what basis? The Ministers&#x2019; Council is part of the Government, after all. They are junior partners, and they themselves govern. Even if it is own affairs, they govern in the most oppressive and disparaging way. They treat us worse than the NP ever did. [Interjections.] In addition, the way in which they govern is moulded according to the Broederbond and the Communist Party&#x2019;s Politburo.</p>

<p>If one does not follow their course, Sir, only one rule applies: Persecute, prosecute and destroy. Sir, the assumed radicalism we are suddenly seeing is merely a disguise for cruel oppression of their fellow man. You toe the line, or else. [Interjections.] It is true what they say: When the mouse has had enough, the meal is bitter. The Ministers&#x2019; Council is proof of this: The mouse has had enough and now the meal is bitter. There is plenty of other evidence that the Ministers&#x2019; Council is forcing the community it is supposed to be serving into the crush-pen of tyranny.</p>

<p>The question, however, is: Is the Ministers&#x2019; Council not an instrument of tyranny itself? It is clear as glass. With the assistance of Government machinery, the Ministers&#x2019; Council is bringing about a new era of slavery. I do not think such a violation of power should be tolerated under any circumstances. Whether it is inside or outside Parliament, this evil of oppression and slavery must be eradicated.</p>

<p>I come back to this once again, and it may sound like a refrain, but I say away with radicalism. These people are nothing but their masters&#x2019; foremen. The radicalism that conjures up so many forebodings is an attempt to soften the community systematically so that they will not oppose further oppression.</p>

<p>The boomerang is striking already, because the silence of the electorate proves that they are not accepting this&#x2014;come what may!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#abrahams">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">L J ABRAHAMS</person>:</from>

<p>You sit in the kitchen drinking tea!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>The hon member for Diamant is welcome to say that I sit in the kitchen drinking tea. Who sat in the palace of Pharaoh for more than two and a half years, and did not merely sit there drinking tea?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>Why does the hon the State President not want to make you a Minister then?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I did not ask the hon the State President to make me a Minister, but the hon member is too stupid to accept that. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>Since education is so important in our history, and because it has degenerated into such a phenomenal crisis &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Did the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition say the hon member for Addo was stupid?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, I withdraw it. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Hon members must please not confuse me any further. Hon members are expected to tell the truth. Everything is recorded in Hansard.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, since education is so important in our history, and since it has degenerated into such a phenomenal crisis as a result of the way in which it has been dealt with and maladministered by the Ministers&#x2019; Council, I now want to take a careful look at this hot potato. The reason for this is simple: Our community will stand or fall by education, despite oppressive measures!</p>

<p>It is an irrefutable fact that the Ministers&#x2019; Council has politicised education excessively. This reactionary conduct has resulted in a seething instability&#x2014;not only in education, but also in the community.</p>

<p>This in itself has led to a reckless rejection of the system as a whole. In brief, the Ministers&#x2019; Council is making enemies for itself and for the whole system.</p>

<p>What is worrying about the whole situation is that polarisation has been overtaken. The feeling towards the system has hardened tenfold. This radicalisation, or calcification, is causing hardening frustration in the community that the Ministers&#x2019; Council is supposed to be serving.</p>

<p>It goes without saying, therefore, that their sudden bravado of radicalism is absolutely false. The hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council&#x2019;s constant repetition that the LP is the majority opposition passes all understanding. [Interjections.] They are part of the system that is acting oppressively. They are governing and there are clear signs that they are co-operating with the security police and giving them information. It is no wonder, because a former member of the security police&#x2014;I add a question mark to this&#x2014;is their organiser in the Transvaal. He speaked into the University of the Western Cape in earlier days. [Interjections.] At present he is a reporter with the SABC&#x2019;s radio service. [Interjections.] I have written to the Director-General. The Labour Party &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>Talk about your party for a change.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I have spoken about my party already, and the hon member could not understand what I was saying, because it was beyond him. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>The LP supports the state of emergency and abuses it in education. Not a word is said about detention without trial, particularly that of children. [Interjections.] Involuntarily the question arises as to whether they condone or approve of this attack on human rights.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#christians">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J W CHRISTIANS</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is misleading the House, because as a member of this party, I serve on the Standing Committee on Justice and I objected to detention without trial.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition will take cognisance of the hon member&#x2019;s statement.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, I take cognisance of it, but I shall continue my speech. [Interjections.] If that is not true, I want to know whether they have visited all the people that are being detained or have visited their families in order to salvage matters. I want to know that.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#christians">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J W CHRISTIANS</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a further point of order: I went and fetched two children from the Victor Verster. How many children has he fetched?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! That is a question; it is not a point of order. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, let us dwell for a moment on the outrages that have been perpetrated by the Ministers&#x2019; Council. Members of the LP must tell whether or not this is nonsense. I merely hope and trust that they will be permitted at the end of the debate to vote freely according to their conscience. [Interjections.] Or are they going to be afraid of being regarded as Carterites? [Interjections.] I have told the hon member that if he knows what a vulture is, he has never &#x2026; [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from><span class="col_333-334" refersTo="page_0189"/>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Hon members must not compare one another with animals. Will the hon member Mr Lockey please withdraw that if he said it, and will the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition also withdraw what he said?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#lockey">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">D LOCKEY</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, I withdraw it.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, I did not say the hon member was a vulture.</p>

<p>In the first place the hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council is expressing no confidence in his own members. Among them are the hon members for Natal Mid-East, the hon member for Berg River, the hon member for Mid Karoo, the hon member for Wentworth and the hon member Mr Douw. Not one of them was appointed as Minister of Education and Culture. No, he had to assume that position himself. I shall explain why he did so. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>What makes it even worse, is that the hon member for Southern Cape did not resign for the sake of the unity of the Labour Party. He resigned for the sake of other members who would have been intimidated by the hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council. [Interjections.] It is general knowledge that that is the case. If it is not, and he resigned for the sake of the Labour Party, what about the cause of the people? [Interjections.]</p>

<p>Now the hon member for Southern Cape is alone, stripped of everything, and politically he has become a &#x201C;stinker&#x201D;. What has happened to the gentlemen who discussed this urgent matter with the hon former Minister, viz the hon the Minister of Local Government, Housing and Agriculture, the hon member for Bishop Lavis, the hon member for Rawsonville, the hon member for Retreat, the hon member for Berg River, the hon member for Strandfontein, the hon member for Bonteheuwel, the hon member for Elsies River, the hon member for Belhar, the hon member for Matroosfontein and Mr Willie Ross of the President&#x2019;s Council? The only thing those people tried to do was to formulate an opinion, within the province of a so-called democratic body, which they wanted to submit.</p>

<p>The campus chaos has not been alleviated, but instead has been fuelled by <i>inter alia</i> the conversion of bursaries into bursary loans and the withholding of bursaries. In addition&#x2014;and this is probably the most scandalous thing to do&#x2014;student councils that actively oppose the system are being dissolved and banned by the action of a repressive Minister. He says he will appoint them himself.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#jenneke">

<from>Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">L J JENNEKE</person>:</from>

<p>[Inaudible.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Northern Cape must contain himself.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, he would not have been sitting here if I had not helped him during the election.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition must proceed and not try to help me to do my job.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>The hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council can now make appointments himself, and thus far he has not appointed a single student council at any school. He knows that that is undemocratic, impractical and contrary to the wishes of the people. By the grace of God, however, student councils are flourishing and increasing constantly.</p>

<p>Thus far my community has regarded nepotism as a word found only in a dictionary. We saw the dictionary definition as meaning the favouring of friends and family.</p>

<p>Sir, the Ministers&#x2019; Council has concretised that meaning of nepotism in the eyes of the general public. All over promotional appointments are made if one is a member of the Labour Party or is friendly with a member of the Labour Party. There is constant proof of this. If a teacher were to oppose the Labour Party in an election and lose, a reappointment in a teaching post would be taboo. That is the truth. [Interjections.] They forced Mr De Bruyn of Zeerust &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#jenneke">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">L J JENNEKE</person>:</from>

<p>On a point of order, Mr Chairman. The statement the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is making is untrue. The man who opposed me is a headmaster today.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, if hon members will listen to me, I will give them the facts. They do not want to listen, however. I have just explained that Mr De Bruyn was told he could get his position back if he became a member of the Labour Party. [Interjections.] What is more, Sir, Mr Stanley Ernest was told during the most recent election in the <span class="col_335-336" refersTo="page_0190"/>Northern Transvaal that if he did not join the Labour Party, he would not get an appointment. [Interjections.] What are they doing, Sir?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Hon members must please give the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition an opportunity to continue his speech. They will have an opportunity to refute the statements made by the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition with facts. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, a White man was appointed to his position. Has affirmative action also gone to the dogs?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>That is non-racial.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Yes, non-racial, but if the White teacher does not toe the line with the Ministers&#x2019; Council, he will be dismissed. [Interjections.] Sir, I want to repeat this: Be a Labourite and your future, whatever it may be, will be assured. If this happened when Mr Ebrahim was still Minister of Education and Culture, it will happen to a much greater extent now. Sir, Idi Amin himself has taken over the education portfolio. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I withdraw that, Sir. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Addo must please resume his seat. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition has already withdrawn that statement. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>The hon member is welcome to leave. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Will the hon the Minister of Health Services and Welfare please resume his seat? It is wrong of the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition to compare the hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council in the House of Representatives with Idi Amin.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I withdrew that statement. The things they say about me are not objectionable. Only I say all the objectionable things. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I shall not permit that. If hon members on this side hear anything that assails the integrity of a person they must draw my attention to it and I shall make them withdraw it.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_ministers_council">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE MINISTERS&#x2019; COUNCIL</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: Was the statement by the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition not a reflection on the Chair? He said nothing said about him was objectionable, but what he said was wrong.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! It is a reflection on the Chair and I appreciate that. I am doing my best to maintain order.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I support you, Mr Chairman. I was merely responding to a remark made by &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon the Leader can draw my attention to anything that is unparliamentary, and I shall react to it. [Interjections.] I appeal to the hon member for Ravensmead to keep quiet.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, the debate is heated &#x2026; [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Does the hon member for Addo want to put a point of order?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, has the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition apologised to the Chair for his statement?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Yes, the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition did so. He may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I knew the debate would become heated; it was so hot yesterday that we had to adjourn. [Interjections.] I find it very strange in this situation that the hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council is a member of the Regional Education Board in Port Elizabeth.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#oosthuizen">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J C OOSTHUIZEN</person>:</from>

<p>He was elected.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>He was appointed by the former Minister <span class="col_337-338" refersTo="page_0191"/>of Education and Culture, the hon member for Southern Cape.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_members">

<from>*<person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBERS</person>:</from>

<p>No!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Now he serves on a regional education board which is going to make a recommendation in respect of an appointment in a promotional position in education. He could not have his way in the regional board, but as a Minister he will be able to make his appointment now.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#wyngaard">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">C A WYNGAARD</person>:</from>

<p>That is part of power.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>That is part of the abuse of power.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Will the hon member for Wuppertal come to order. One expects hon members to co-operate when one requests that order be maintained. Just for the sake of clarity I want to ask the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition whether the regulations prohibit a Minister from serving on such a regional board.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>That is not my problem, Sir. My problem</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_ministers_council">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE MINISTERS&#x2019; COUNCIL</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is misleading the House. I served on the committee as an elected member before I was appointed to the Education and Culture portfolio by the hon the State President.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Thank you very much. That clears up the situation.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>The hon the Leader must stop &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I am appealing to the hon member for Addo for the second time to come to order! The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I have no problem with what is determined by the regulations. To me this principle is the issue: How can a Minister who has executive authority serve on an advisory body which has to advise that same Minister? That is my dilemma, and that is the dilemma of education in general, Sir.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#oosthuizen">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J C OOSTHUIZEN</person>:</from>

<p>You do not even have a mandate to sit there in the opposition benches. Why do you not resign from your seat here? What dilemma &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Would the hon member for Swartland please come to order. Thank you very much. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>All these appointments are being made merely to &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#oosthuizen">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J C OOSTHUIZEN</person>:</from>

<p>You should resign your seat.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>&#x2026; oblige the Ministers&#x2019; Council and they negate the future and the catalysing effect on our community.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#morkel">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">G N MORKEL</person>:</from>

<p>That is an anglicism.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>If the hon member knows what an anglicism is, he has spoken English before. [Interjections.] Permit me to illustrate this by means of an example. I should then like to know what the hon member for Retreat has to say. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#morkel">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">G N MORKEL</person>:</from>

<p>I shall say you are a &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Retreat is not to act in this way. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is provoking the hon member and he is reacting to this. I shall not permit it. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I shall start with the latest chaotic incident. At Manenberg Senior Secondary School a brilliant &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#oosthuizen">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J C OOSTHUIZEN</person>:</from>

<p>But that is just gossip. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>You see, Sir, the hon member calls that gossip. Such is their reprehensible conduct, but I am not permitted to react because then I am told I am offending them.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! It is not unparliamentary to call something &#x201C;gossip&#x201D; because the reference is to an object&#x2014;it does not affect a person.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from><span class="col_339-340" refersTo="page_0192"/>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I am with you, Sir.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Then the hon Leader should accept that I am in the right.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Yes, Sir, but I am addressing the hon member and not you, Sir. Allow me to explain my meaning. If they call this gossip, they would do better to tell me it is untrue.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Yes, that is correct.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>It is the subject of the debate after all.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#oosthuizen">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J C OOSTHUIZEN</person>:</from>

<p>We have already called it gossip.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>All right, then that hon Minister should tell me this is so, but permit me to tell the story.</p>

<p>A brilliant teacher was appointed to the principalship of the Manenberg Secondary School a day before it reopened. The man had no chance of settling in or mapping out a strategy for his school. The Ministers&#x2019; Council is perfectly aware that Manenberg schools are creating problems. A chaotic situation arose at the school involved which spread rapidly to other schools in the vicinity. That once brilliant man is a wreck today. Nevertheless the Ministers&#x2019; Council neglects to address problems caused by their dragging their feet over appointments. The closing date for applications is June; why should a man be appointed only the day before? If one confronts them with this in the corridors, they ask why we cannot do it if the Afrikaner can. That is the type of reply one receives. This may sound like a refrain but I shall repeat it: How can the Ministers&#x2019; Council be radical and act in opposition while using all the instruments of oppression to plough the community under? [Interjections.]</p>

<p>Sir, let us take another look at the nepotism applied by the Ministers&#x2019; Council. We are faced with the most horrendous situation: Belong to the Labour Party or be in sympathy with them and you will be all right. There is endless delay in making appointments at schools. Professional people in the department are ignored totally while the hon the Minister concerned and his two clerks have to deal with the entire education portfolio. Why is there such procrastination?</p>

<p>Hon members could well ask the hon the Minister to produce the letter.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#koeberg">

<from>Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">C KOEBERG</person>:</from>

<p>[Inaudible.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, there is so much delay because each applicant has to be screened for security reasons.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#lockey">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">D LOCKEY</person>:</from>

<p>Only the Black people.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, it applies to all teachers.</p>

<p>&#x2020;If that does not happen, there is once again prosecution, persecution and ultimately complete destruction.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#herandien">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">C B HERANDIEN</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: The hon member for Northern Cape said the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition &#x201C;moet sy bek warm praat&#x201D;.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Did the hon member for Northern Cape say that?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#jenneke">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">L J JENNEKE</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I did not say that. I am sitting here at the back.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I did not hear it but I ask again whether the hon member said that. I shall request a Hansard transcription and, if the hon member did say so, I shall have no choice but to take steps against him.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I wish to refer again to the nepotism of the recent non-appointments and late appointments. I have already referred to the teacher, a Mr Morrison, who was appointed a day before schools were to reopen. White teachers are dismissed and according to my information it is because they do not pay lip service to the Labour Party. Whites are also appointed to replace Coloured people merely because they do not genuflect to the Ministers&#x2019; Council. Mr Ernest is a telling example of this.</p>

<p>After a personal call by the hon the Leader of the Ministers&#x2019; Council to induce Mr Nick Wagenstroom to join the LP&#x2014;which he did not do&#x2014;he was not appointed to a post for which he had been recommended by the school committee, the regional council and the selection committee of the department. Does that represent opposition or the oppressive execution of government functions?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_ministers_council">

<from><span class="col_341-342" refersTo="page_0193"/>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE MINISTERS&#x2019; COUNCIL</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is repeating blatant lies; they are devoid of all truth. His statements are not founded on facts and I shall correct them later in the debate.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Is the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition sure of the facts he is mentioning?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I am putting them to the House and asking them for their reaction because &#x2026; [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council acted correctly so it is my duty to request the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition and every other hon member to make sure of their facts, because a member is expected to speak only the truth in spite of the immunity we have here. That is why I requested the Hansard version. I have to act in accordance with the Standing Orders. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition in this House is continually referring to nepotism.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! I cannot permit the hon member to address me on just any matter when he puts a point of order.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>I am putting a point of order, Sir. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! All right, then put it.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>The definition in the dictionary reads as follows:</p>

<block name="quote">Nepotism is the appointment of one&#x2019;s relatives</block>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! That is not a point of order.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>But that is misleading. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! If the hon member says it is misleading, he has the right to say so.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I merely wish to put the record straight.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, as I understand it, the dictionary defines nepotism as the favouring of relatives and friends. We can fetch one right now.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! No, leave the dictionary. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed; just let him make sure of the facts. He has freedom of speech but I remind him that I appealed to all hon members to make certain of their facts in the knowledge that everything they said here could be verified.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I can submit a document to you, Sir&#x2014;not to them because they will not understand it&#x2014; containing the facts I have presented here in the House. I am not sucking them out of my thumb.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>Then where are you sucking them from?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I have given it to the Chairman of the House; that hon member has nothing further to do with it. If he feels so strongly about relatives not being favoured in politics, what is he doing in this House? [Interjections.] What is his brother-in-law doing in this House? [Interjections.] No, I am not afraid of being tackled by that hon member. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! Please let us leave personal remarks out of this. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#lockey">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">D LOCKEY</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is well known for his reprehensible conduct. I merely want to point out to him &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#lockey">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">D LOCKEY</person>:</from>

<p>I withdraw that, Sir. I merely want to point out to him that I was not the hon member for Addo&#x2019;s brother-in-law when I came to this House. I was married of my own free will. In addition I am still with my wife; he is no longer with his. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member Mr Lockey had better withdraw that statement. [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#lockey">

<from><span class="col_343-344" refersTo="page_0194"/>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">D LOCKEY</person>:</from>

<p>Sir, I refuse to withdraw it because the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is no longer living with his wife.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! In that case I order the hon member Mr Lockey, having disregarded the authority of the Chair, to withdraw from the Chamber for the remainder of the day&#x2019;s sitting.</p>

<p>[Whereupon the member withdrew.]</p>

<p>The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>That is all they can use against me, Sir.</p>

<p>As regards Nick Wagenstroom, I realise the most disturbing aspect was that security police subjected various members of the school committee to a searching interrogation merely because they had recommended Mr Nick Wagenstroom. My question to the Ministers&#x2019; Council is: Who instructed this to be done? [Interjections.] I wrote to him and I am not ashamed of this.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>Let the security police interrogate people before they can get houses.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I did not use the word &#x201C;interrogate&#x201D;.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hendrickse">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">P A C HENDRICKSE</person>:</from>

<p>Yes!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Addo will also be given the opportunity of putting his case. The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition may proceed.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>Ignore them! They are irrelevant!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>One of the members of my party has just instructed me to ignore these hon members entirely so I shall do this.</p>

<p>Mr Chairman, Mr H M Daniels of Bredasdorp was denied promotion. [Interjections.] Nobody uses me except my constituents!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>Except PW.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>When did the hon member last visit his constituency?</p>

<p>Mr Chairman, Mr Daniels merely exercised his democratic right by criticising a certain letter. In this letter pensioners were threatened with the possible withdrawal of their pensions if they did not attend a meeting of the hon the Minister of Health Services and Welfare. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>Mr M Soeker was also recommended by the school committee, the regional council and the professional selection committee of the department but he was not appointed either, because this time it was the hon member for Bonteheuwel who had whispered to the Minister that Mr Soeker sold <i>Grassroots</i> at the Bonteheuwel school. Sir, what has democracy become?</p>

<p>The school committee, the regional council and the professional selection committee recommended him, so what constitutes merit? Are we merely to have a group of puppets in our schools who will lower our educational standard to such an extent that we shall all ultimately be on the losing side?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#oosthuizen">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J C OOSTHUIZEN</person>:</from>

<p>One cannot appoint a newspaper vendor to the principalship of a school!</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Oh, but it does not matter if <i>Steadfast</i> is distributed at schools! [Interjections.] It does not matter that dozens of copies of <i>Steadfast</i> are distributed at schools! [Interjections.] Now the hon member is talking about a newspaper vendor!</p>

<p>Mr Chairman, Mr A J Februarie was similarly not appointed as the deputy headmaster at Bergzicht Primary School. Can hon members imagine what detrimental effects such a politicised action can have on education? Elected, not nominated, student councils want absolutely nothing to do with the department. If one speaks to a high school student, especially in the Peninsula, one dare not admit to being a member of Parliament because one would be in hot water in consequence of the action of one of these junior partners of the hon the State President.</p>

<p>Mr Chairman, why do you think the former Director of Education resigned his post before reaching retirement age? He could no longer tolerate the exasperation caused by political interference. [Interjections.]</p>

<p>Teachers who do serve on such committees by invitation are purely used as window-dressing. The community regards them as traitors without <span class="col_345-346" refersTo="page_0195"/>a mandate, speaking only on behalf of themselves without the consent of the corps of teachers and parents. Over and above the fact that friends receive appointments in education, this penetrates even to school committees. There are no quorums when school committees have to be elected and then only members of the LP are appointed to such committees. Regional councils are overloaded with these cultus figures who stand so remote from their own community.</p>

<p>The most disgraceful action of the Ministers&#x2019; Council is probably the withdrawal of stop-order facilities for membership of teachers&#x2019; associations. Thanks to a little perspicacity that withdrawal was cancelled. What a mess we land ourselves in.</p>

<p>An ever greater thorn in one&#x2019;s flesh must be the Kairos case in Oudtshoorn. [Interjections.] Quite democratically, spontaneously and prompted by conviction and love of their fellows, people gathered to provide retarded children with education. But what happened? They were well organised and applied for a subsidy which was refused unless they substituted a different name for Kairos. Such is the Ministers&#x2019; Council. Representations were then made to the Minister-&#x2014;perhaps I should not hold this against the former Minister, now dismissed, but he was part of the scheme of things&#x2014;and he gave a firm undertaking that the subsidy would be paid regardless of the name.</p>

<p>When the hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council heard this, he said: &#x201C;Over my dead body. The name Kairos is to be changed or there will be no subsidy.&#x201D; They must tell me whether that subsidy has been paid to Kairos to date.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>You can find out.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I know very well it has not, which is why I am giving the hon member the facts. [Interjections.] Does this not represent a classic example of the violation of democracy? I not only find it strange but also quite peculiar &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#minister_of_health_services_and_welfare">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">MINISTER OF HEALTH SERVICES AND WELFARE</person>:</from>

<p>Amusing.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>When I use the word &#x201C;amusing&#x201D;, the hon the Minister calls it an anglicism. That is why I say it is peculiar that, when the hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council wanted to call streets after Mandela and Sisulu and the Government would not permit it, he became as depressed as Genis and lodged protest after protest. Why act out of prejudice or opposition towards Kairos now? All this tells me &#x2026;</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#swigelaar">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J D SWIGELAAR</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, on a point of order: The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is misleading this House as regards Kairos.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>Has the subsidy been paid?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon member for Dysselsdorp said the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition was misleading the House. What did he mean by that? [Interjections.]</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#swigelaar">

<from>*Mr <person refersTo="hansard_za">J D SWIGELAAR</person>:</from>

<p>Mr Chairman, I shall make a point of obtaining a turn to speak in this debate to refute, step by step, allegations made by the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>All I shall ultimately want to know is whether there were objections to Kairos and whether the subsidy was paid. I am not interested in the hon member&#x2019;s step-by-step tale.</p>

<p>All this tells me is that the Labour Party together with its Ministers&#x2019; Council is a confused Jonas. The hon the Chairman of the Ministers&#x2019; Council ostensibly resigned from the Cabinet but his colleagues all remained members of the State President&#x2019;s Cabinet Committee, so that resignation was merely a transparent trick. It was irrelevant to the community because he now becomes the Minister of Education and Culture for own affairs. This is nothing but a rift between Jonas and his master Peter on the farm. Jonas pretends to be angry but continues living in the hut on the farm. Jonas is angry about the tractor his master Peter refused to lend him but he remains in the hut without protest. At this stage the hut is in Reigersdal but they now want to move to other huts in Walmer Estate. The shocking aspect is that the Ministers&#x2019; Council now constitutes the latest resettlement in terms of the Group Areas Act which they are so frantic to abolish. Does the Labour Party accept that its Ministers&#x2019; Council has to be moved to a Coloured area under the Group Areas Act? If that is not so, why the blackmail about group areas?</p>

<p>The hon the Minister of Local Government, <span class="col_347-348" refersTo="page_0196"/>Housing and Agriculture has my sympathy. He says his colleagues request him to build houses although they want the Group Areas Act abolished. Where is he to build houses in such a case?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>That is a mystery.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>It is a mystery.</p>

<p>My only conviction is that the Labour Party has discovered new toys which will explode in their faces.</p>

<p>&#x2020;It is so true, and they have said it so many times that I want to repeat it: You can bluff some of the people most of the time, but you can&#x2019;t bluff all the people all the time. How the hell can they now say &#x201C;ahoy&#x201D;? (Interjections.)</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#chairman_of_the_house">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">CHAIRMAN OF THE HOUSE</person>:</from>

<p>Order! The hon the Leader of the Official Opposition is not to use the expression &#x201C;how the hell&#x201D;.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>I withdraw it, Sir.</p>

<p>The former high priest of Black consciousness will have his turn soon. I attached significance to metamorphosis before, but I really did not expect such a change this time. His actions would leave a chameleon wondering where the name originated. Instead of merely supplying houses, he is mixed up in Labour Party politics.</p>

<p>The hon the Minister of Local Government, Housing and Agriculture is someone who persistently requests memoranda. All we receive from him in return, however, is a vague meaningless bureaucratic reply. When I was still a member of the Labour Party, the Transvaal caucus submitted a comprehensive memorandum to him as regards the housing situation in the Transvaal. To date they have not received a proper reply to it. [Interjections.] No, I am not mentioning isolated incidents but the overall situation in the Transvaal.</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#hon_member">

<from>*An <person refersTo="hansard_za">HON MEMBER</person>:</from>

<p>Did the hon the Leader of the Official Opposition receive a reply?</p>

</speech>

<speech by="#leader_of_the_official_opposition">

<from>*The <person refersTo="hansard_za">LEADER OF THE OFFICIAL OPPOSITION</person>:</from>

<p>It is even worse that no clear housing strategy has been designed for the Transvaal yet. My constituents have reason to ask why we do not follow the lead of the hon the Chairman of the House in the House of Delegates. Every five minutes he announces where he is building houses and where he intends building them. [Interjections.] The question arises: What is the programme as regards housing for this country? South Africa awaits the reply. I shall be the first person to congratulate the hon the Minister if he should announce such a programme.</p>

<p>In last year&#x2019;s debate I warned the hon the Minister not to politicise housing but he remains blind and deaf to this. One has to be a member of the Labour Party to obtain funds for this purpose.
